# Weekly Link & Crawl Sweep — 2026-11-24

**Role:** seo-auditor · **Scope:** WEEKLY (CADENCE.md §3.3 — Link & crawl sweep, Tuesdays 14:00 UTC)
**Base:** production `main` @ `8e33aea` · **Build:** `npm run build` ✅ **87 pages** (58 posts, 26 tag pages, homepage, 2 author/about pages)
**Checks:** **1941** (rule, URL) evaluations across **40** of the 41 SEO-PLAYBOOK rules; **0 unevaluable** (SD-02 has no in-scope URL — no published post carries `updated` frontmatter this cycle).
**Gate:** `none` for this report; `human-merge` for any PR.
**Invariants honored:** no deploy · no merge · no `main` edit · no client-side JS · `llms.txt` canon untouched · `WAEV_PREVIEW` unset · checked for an existing report/PR for the 2026-11-24 slot first (none found).

> Methodology: the built `dist/` HTML was evaluated, not source. JSON-LD was extracted from `<script type="application/ld+json">` blocks and `JSON.parse`-d; meta/link/anchor/heading checks parse the built HTML; the `.prose` body container is isolated by `<div>`-depth matching so FAQ and RelatedPosts links are correctly excluded from IL-02, and the `.related-posts` section is isolated the same way for IL-01. Full per-(rule,URL) JSON result set is appended at the bottom for the audit trail (CADENCE.md §3.3/§3.6).
> Built on Node 22 (the environment did not provide the `.nvmrc` Node 24). Astro 5 emits the same static `dist/`; the audit is unaffected.

## Week-over-week: no change

Production `main` is **unchanged since the 2026-11-17 sweep** (both based on `8e33aea`), and this run reproduces that sweep's verdicts **exactly**: the same 3 rules fail on the same URLs, every other rule passes. No new regression, no newly-resolved finding, no slug-set drift. The verdict set has now held steady across the 2026-11-03, 2026-11-10, 2026-11-17 and 2026-11-24 sweeps.

## FAILs this cycle (3 rules) — and why none need a new fix

| Rule | Severity | # | Disposition |
|------|----------|---|-------------|
| **MT-02** | blocker | 8 | Meta descriptions > 160 chars. **All 8 are already trimmed in open draft PRs #86 (7 posts) + #87 (`the-map-you-didnt-draw`)** — both `mergeable_state: clean` (re-verified this run). Re-fixing here would duplicate in-flight work and create a competing draft — not done (CADENCE.md §0.5 idempotency). They clear on human merge of those PRs. |
| **IL-02** | major | 7 | Fewer than 2 inline prose cross-links. **Non-mechanical** (authoring-time editorial — the auditor never edits post prose). Tracked in open issue **#85**; the current 7-post set is **identical** to the set recorded there (narrowed to 7 on 2026-09-29), so a continuity comment is posted for the 2026-11-24 cycle rather than a new issue or an edit. |
| **HP-04** | minor | 1 | `the-opinionated-mesh-analyzer`: value-card `<h3>`s render above the body `<h2>`s (heading sequence jumps `1 → 3`) — component-driven, not post prose. **Reported only** (minor). |

## Link & crawl health — all green

- **IL-03** (no broken internal links): **PASS** on all 87 pages. No published post is future-dated this cycle (all 58 are live), so there is **no IL-03 scheduled-post forward-reference carve-out** — every internal `href` resolves to a built path in `dist/`.
- **IL-04** (no orphans): **PASS** — every post has in-degree ≥ 1.
- **IL-01** (RelatedPosts block): **PASS** on all 58 — each post renders a `.related-posts` section with 2–4 non-self `/blog/<slug>/` links.
- **MT-03** (self-canonical): **PASS** on every page. **SM-02**: every sitemap URL equals its page canonical (no host/slash drift).
- **SD-11** (JSON-LD valid, no placeholders): **PASS** — all `application/ld+json` blocks parse; no empty/`undefined`/`null`/`TODO`/relative-where-absolute values.
- **SD-06** (Org `sameAs` not the source repo): **PASS** — `sameAs` is omitted entirely (the correct default); no `github.com/*/waev-blog` identity claim.
- **SD-10** (CollectionPage on tag pages): **PASS** on all 26 — each page's `CollectionPage` enumerates exactly its tagged posts via `hasPart` (name `Posts tagged "<tag>"`, canonical `url`).
- **SM-01/03/04** — sitemap indexes all 85 public URLs (58 posts + 26 tags + home) with no draft/future leakage; `llms.txt` lists exactly the 58 published posts; `llms.txt` Key-facts canon substrings all present.
- All of **SD-01/03/04/05/07/08/09**, **TG-01–05**, **MT-01/04/05/06/07/08/09/10**, **HP-01/02/03**, **IL-05**, **SM-05/06** **PASS** on every in-scope URL.

## Outbound links — liveness verified this cycle

This environment's agent proxy reaches the public internet, so outbound liveness was checked. **15 distinct outbound content URLs** across 13 hosts (excluding `blog.waev.app`, `schema.org`, `w3.org`, and the Google Fonts CSS/infra origins).

- **Live (HTTP 200, 11 URLs):** `config.meshcore.io`; `docs.fcc.gov` (DA-12-839A1.pdf); `github.com/Treehouse-00/waev-blog`; `map.meshcore.io`; `meshcore.io/flasher`; `meshtastic.org` (LoRa_Design_Guide PDF); `mosquitto.org/man/mosquitto-tls-7.html`; `stuff.cisien.com/fcc-900mhz-regulations.html`; `www.ecfr.gov` §15.247; `www.ecfr.gov` §15.249; `www.mouser.com` (AN120062 PDF).
- **HTTP 403 — WAF/bot-challenge, not defects (3 hosts):**
  - `letsmesh.net/` and `meshmapper.net/` — both linked from `four-tools-one-mesh`; a 403 (not NXDOMAIN/timeout/404) means the host is up and deliberately refusing automated clients, so the pages are almost certainly live for ordinary browser visitors. Same observation as the 2026-10-27, 2026-11-03, 2026-11-10 and 2026-11-17 sweeps. Rewriting an outbound link on a WAF 403 is an editorial judgment the auditor does not make mechanically — **no fix made**.
  - `waev.app` (and `/outpost`) — Waev's **own first-party product site**, behind the same WAF behavior. Not a broken-link defect; it is the canonical product domain and is live for browsers. No fix.
- A browser-UA recheck to confirm the WAF behavior was intentionally **not** attempted — bot-check circumvention is out of bounds.

## Human-judgment items (unchanged from prior cycles)

- **#86 / #87** — the MT-02 trims remain **unmerged open drafts** (both `mergeable_state: clean`). Merging both clears all 8 MT-02 blockers.
- **#85** — IL-02 editorial prose work across 7 posts; continuity comment posted for the 2026-11-24 cycle, set unchanged since 2026-09-29.
- **MT-07** (`twitter:site`): **no action** — omission is the ratified passing state (SEO-PLAYBOOK §2, ratified 2026-07-04; Waev has no official handle). Not a `human-approval` finding.
- **SM-04** canon intact, **SD-06** passes — **no `human-approval` findings this run**.

## Disposition

No new safe mechanical fix exists this cycle: every blocker/major mechanical violation is already pending in an open draft PR (#86/#87), and the only open `major` not covered by a PR (IL-02) is non-mechanical and tracked in issue #85. This report is therefore **report-only** — gate `none`. A human may merge it to land the audit trail.
